## `AGO-4630-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Pallet-Shuttle-Freezer Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Food Cold-Storage AS/RS Temperature Zoning
- **Intro:** Pallet-Shuttle-Freezer Operator — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Food Cold-Storage AS/RS Temperature Zoning — Pre-Shift Authorization and PPE Verification: O2: 20.8-21.0% (alarm at 19.5%); NH3: <25 ppm (alarm at 35 ppm); PPE must be free of tears and rated to -50°C.
